**第50讲  外接球、内切球、棱切球**

![](images/fe66afbd7d70f8d45c8c353a16091812229252615cf6ad7365cf2ec675160669.jpg)

![](images/f9638525faa5fdc9911ef0c491bca869166372e19a7d6f16b4169a09cf5974d4.jpg)

![](images/dfa7d99e47ac3d9020ca1f88ae8bf5be3708d6d8815485119bc5f11a786df3fa.jpg)

![](images/62a646798799cb3c683f8c21aa735e7c5fd943f574c9d3fe736f7be882c286d2.jpg)

**知识梳理**

**知识点一：正方体、长方体外接球**

1、正方体的外接球的球心为其体对角线的中点，半径为体对角线长的一半．

2、长方体的外接球的球心为其体对角线的中点，半径为体对角线长的一半．

3、补成长方体

（1）若三棱锥的三条侧棱两两互相垂直，则可将其放入某个长方体内，如图1所示．

（2）若三棱锥的四个面均是直角三角形，则此时可构造长方体，如图2所示．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

（3）正四面体可以补形为正方体且正方体的棱长，如图3所示．

（4）若三棱锥的对棱两两相等，则可将其放入某个长方体内，如图4所示

![](images/d20e364be9ea8e7feff18dc56c275b0300cbec6a69f94f792b6cd4443b4122a6.jpg)  
图1          图2              图3             图4

**知识点二：正四面体外接球**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图，设正四面体的的棱长为，将其放入正方体中，则正方体的棱长为，显然正四面体和正方体有相同的外接球．正方体外接球半径为，即正四面体外接球半径为．

![](images/f5b1621f018934f9b40ea600c5829415d60c059183fa292396b87ca617f6e375.jpg)

**知识点三：对棱相等的三棱锥外接球**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

四面体中，，，，这种四面体叫做对棱相等四面体，可以通过构造长方体来解决这类问题．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图，设长方体的长、宽、高分别为，则，三式相加可得而显然四面体和长方体有相同的外接球，设外接球半径为，则，所以．

![](images/40e34518b93108648b521bceb9c38f764484c997a1d8c52f4edef76585f809c0.jpg)

**知识点四：直棱柱外接球**

如图1，图2，图3，直三棱柱内接于球（同时直棱柱也内接于圆柱，棱柱的上下底面可以是任意三角形）

**知识点五：直棱锥外接球**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图，平面，求外接球半径．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

解题步骤：

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

第一步：将画在小圆面上，为小圆直径的一个端点，作小圆的直径，连接，则必过球心；

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

第二步：为的外心，所以平面，算出小圆的半径(三角形的外接圆直径算法：利用正弦定理，得)，；

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

第三步：利用勾股定理求三棱锥的外接球半径：①；

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

②．

**知识点六：正棱锥与侧棱相等模型**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

**1、正棱锥外接球半径： ．**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

2、**侧棱相等模型：**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图，的射影是的外心

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

三棱锥的三条侧棱相等

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

三棱锥的底面在圆锥的底上，顶点点也是圆锥的顶点．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

解题步骤：

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

第一步：确定球心的位置，取的外心，则三点共线；

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

第二步：先算出小圆的半径，再算出棱锥的高（也是圆锥的高）；

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

第三步：勾股定理：，解出．

**知识点七：侧棱为外接球直径模型**

方法：找球心，然后作底面的垂线，构造直角三角形．

**知识点八：共斜边拼接模型**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图，在四面体中，，，此四面体可以看成是由两个共斜边的直角三角形拼接而形成的，为公共的斜边，故以“共斜边拼接模型”命名之．设点为公共斜边的中点，根据直角三角形斜边中线等于斜边的一半的结论可知，，即点到，，，四点的距离相等，故点就是四面体外接球的球心，公共的斜边就是外接球的一条直径．

![](images/b727eedcb40d3d8a19de7208f0bef9888acc3e1487bc518b2f6509b8595ef7ff.png)

**知识点九：垂面模型**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图1所示为四面体，已知平面平面，其外接球问题的步骤如下：

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

（1）找出和的外接圆圆心，分别记为和．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

（2）分别过和作平面和平面的垂线，其交点为球心，记为．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

（3）过作的垂线，垂足记为，连接，则．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

（4）在四棱锥中，垂直于平面，如图2所示，底面四边形的四个顶点共圆且为该圆的直径．

![](images/48ca2607e09dea0a2f4c6a991ae7826ba243076f4abb902f9a296e76c7a6ce25.jpg)

![](images/f578ead7155df3f83787d8ce955a27fc622285c77756d37f782ea580dd5cfe5c.jpg)

**知识点十：最值模型**

这类问题是综合性问题，方法较多，常见方法有：导数法，基本不等式法，观察法等

**知识点十一：二面角模型**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

如图1所示为四面体，已知二面角大小为，其外接球问题的步骤如下：

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

（1）找出和的外接圆圆心，分别记为和．

**知识点十二：坐标法**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

对于一般多面体的外接球，可以建立空间直角坐标系，设球心坐标为，利用球心到各顶点的距离相等建立方程组，解出球心坐标，从而得到球的半径长．坐标的引入，使外接球问题的求解从繁琐的定理推论中解脱出来，转化为向量的计算，大大降低了解题的难度．

**题型二：外接球之正四面体模型**

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

<strong>例4．</strong>（2024·湖北宜昌·宜昌市夷陵中学校考模拟预测）已知正四面体<em>ABCD</em>的表面积为，且<em>A</em>，<em>B</em>，<em>C</em>，<em>D</em>四点都在球<em>O</em>的球面上，则球<em>O</em>的体积为______．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

所以该正四面体的表面积为，所以，

又正方体的面对角线可构成正四面体，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

若正四面体棱长为，可得正方体的棱长为1，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

所以正方体的外接球即为该正四面体的外接球，所以外接球的直径为，半径为，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

所以球*O*的体积为．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

故答案为：

解得，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

**题型九：外接球之垂面模型**

<strong>例25．</strong>（2024·江西九江·高一校考期末）如图，三棱锥中，平面平面<em>BCD</em>，是边长为2的等边三角形，，.若<em>A</em>，<em>B</em>，<em>C</em>，<em>D</em>四点在某个球面上，则该球体的表面积为______.

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

【解析】作出底面的外心，侧面的外心，取中点，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

连接，因为平面平面，面平面，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

因为是边长为2的等边三角形，所以，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

又因为平面，所以平面，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

由球的性质可得平面，所以，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

在中，因为，，则，

设的外接圆半径为，根据正弦定理有，则，

取*BC*中点*G*，连接*AG,DG*，则，，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

【解析】因为，所以的外接圆圆心即点，三棱锥外接球球心在过点与平面垂直的直线上，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

由正弦定理得，所以，所以三棱锥的外接球的表面积为.

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

因为正方体的内切球直径等于正方体的棱长，所以正方体的棱长为，故正方体的体积为.

故选：D.

**变式60．**（2024·全国·高一专题练习）若一个正三棱柱存在外接球与内切球，则它的外接球与内切球体积之比为（    ）

A．	B．	C．	D．

【答案】C

【解析】设正三棱柱底面正三角形的边长为*a*，则正三棱柱的内切球半径等于正三角形的内切圆半

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

径，则内切球的半径，正三棱柱的高．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

设正三角形的外接圆半径为*R*，易得，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

所以外接球的半径．

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

所以它的外接球与内切球体积之比为．

故选：C

<strong>变式63．</strong>（2024·全国·高三专题练习）已知点<em>O</em>到直三棱柱各面的距离都相等，球<em>O</em>是直三棱柱的内切球，若球<em>O</em>的表面积为，的周长为4，则三棱锥的体积为（    ）

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

A．	B．	C．	D．

【答案】B

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

【解析】设直三棱柱的高为*h*，*AB*＝*c*，*BC*＝*a*，*AC*＝*b*，内切球*O*的半径为*r*，则*h*＝2*r*，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

由题意可知球*O*的表面积为，解得*r*＝2，∴*h*＝4，

又△*ABC*的周长为4，即*a*＋*b*＋*c*＝4，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

∴连接*OA*，*OB*，*OC*，可将直三棱柱分成5个棱锥，

即三个以原来三棱柱侧面为底面，内切球球心为顶点的四棱锥，

两个以原来三棱柱底面为底面，内切球球心为顶点的的三棱锥，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

∴由体积相等可得直三棱柱的体积为*h*＝*ahr*＋*bhr*＋*chr*＋2×*r*，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

即4＝（*a*＋*b*＋*c*）*hr*＋，∴＝，

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

∴三棱锥的体积为*h*＝×4×4＝．

故选：B．

![](images/d7a30c83c0186352ef66fa86d9555cbfda08d4f8d44fe7cc873b976f120f4dad.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

为的中心，易知面，为中点，球和球分别与面相切于和.

【解析】如图1所示，正四面体*ABCD*中，*AH*⊥底面*BCD*，*E*、*F*、*G*、*K*为四个球的球心，*M*为*CD*中点，连接*BM*，*AM*，易知*B*、*H*、*M*三点共线，直线*AH*交平面*EFG*于点，连接，交*GF*于点*N*，则*N*为*GF*的中点，因为内切球半径为2，故*EF*=4，画出截面*ABM*如图2所示，正四棱锥*EFGK*外接球球心设为*O*，则正四面体*ABCD*的外接球球心与正四面体*EFGK*外接球球心重合，设正四面体*ABCD*的外接球半径为*R*，正四面体*EFGK*外接球半径为*r*，在图2中，*EK*=4，，，，所以

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)

由，即，解得：

![](images/5e383d1d45b7de4b9fa9143e4e9ea41713e055a9d72ac00614059c40393f341c.jpg)
